点击查看摘要

Abstract:Chunking strategies for retrieval-augmented generation are almost always evaluated on a static snapshot, yet the documents underlying a knowledge base continue to change. Because chunks are embedded independently, a boundary that moves after an edit forces unchanged text to be re-embedded. We measure this update cost on 120 pairs of adjacent Chinese and English Wikipedia revisions for three stateless chunkers (non-overlapping fixed-size, recursive paragraph-first, and content-defined chunking) under a strict byte-identical reuse rule and a frozen dense encoder. On the 116-article common-success pool, with frozen, length-calibrated parameters, fixed-size chunking recomputes 57-68% of its full new-version embedding-input workload, recursive chunking 38-56%, and content-defined chunking 43-54% (it failed on 4 of 62 Chinese articles), while at these parameters mean evidence recall under a fixed 2048-token retrieval budget differs by less than two percentage points within either language. A cost-only parameter grid shows that the ranking of the two content-aware chunkers changes with chunk-length settings, whereas non-overlapping fixed-size chunking has the highest recomputation cost in every length-matched condition. Controlled edits and a verbatim-presence diagnostic support boundary drift as one contributor to recomputation of text already present in the previous version. Update cost is therefore a substantial, parameter-sensitive dimension that static retrieval evaluations do not expose and that should be reported alongside retrieval quality.

6. 【2610.30941】PAIR: Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval for Premium People Recommendation at LinkedIn

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30941

作者：Zhongkai Shangguan,Yanbin Kang,Yihan Cao,Ruijie Jiang,Qiannan Yin,Chang Yue,Zijian Zhou,Girish Kathalagiri,Changshuai Wei,Zhengming Xing

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:General-purpose embeddings trained on profile similarity often misrank candidates in professional networks, where relevance is inherently complementary rather than symmetric. For example, job seekers usually engage with recruiters rather than peers with similar credentials. Current user recommendation systems are often built on profile similarity or social signals such as shared connections, which are effective in friend-oriented social networks where users tend to connect with similar people or within existing social circles. However, this does not hold in professional platforms like LinkedIn, where interactions are often goal-driven and cross-role interactions, requiring matching based on complementary intents rather than similarity or social proximity. In this paper, we propose Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval, a retrieval framework that is designed to model user intent preference instead of profile similarity. Our approach is inspired by the preference-learning perspective of Direct Preference Optimization, but instantiates preference learning as an exposure-conditioned multi-negative contrastive objective over embeddings. This formulation simplifies training and scales efficiently to large candidate sets without requiring an explicit reference model. Furthermore, we incorporate user interaction history by aggregating click histories with positional encoding to capture temporal order, which enables constant-cost updates of user representations over a fixed history window, allowing new interactions to be incorporated without re-encoding history. As a result, personalization can be updated efficiently using lightweight CPU operations without repeated LLM inference during serving. We evaluate PAIR on LinkedIn's Premium retrieval task. A 0.6B parameter model achieves a MAR@100 of 0.45.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30825

作者：Ziang Lu,Yu Zhang,Xinzhe Jiang,Lei Sang,Yiwen Zhang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Cross-domain sequential recommendation aims to alleviate data sparsity and cold-start problems by transferring useful preference knowledge across domains. However, users exhibit varying degrees of preference consistency across domains, while existing methods often rely on global alignment objectives or coarse-grained transfer strategies. Such approaches may introduce irrelevant information and provide limited flexibility in adapting semantic representations to individual user preferences. To address these challenges, we propose BGAFlow, a personalized framework that combines state-adaptive semantic evolution with user-specific transfer control. BGAFlow first establishes a shared semantic space through unified dual-domain pretraining with user profiles inferred by a large language model (LLM). Based on latent interest overlap, it then estimates a personalized horizon to determine the appropriate transfer extent for each user. To enable semantic adaptation, BGAFlow constructs a conditional Bezier curve as a geometric reference and learns a residual flow that progressively refines the trajectory based on intermediate preference states. The learned flow incorporates dynamic preference feedback to adjust transfer directions, while recommendation supervision and trajectory regularization encourage effective and smooth adaptation. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that BGAFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the benefits of personalized transfer control and state-adaptive semantic evolution.

11. 【2610.30697】Lychee: Certified Screening of Inverted Lists for High-Recall Vector Search

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30697

作者：Dongfang Zhao

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：probed lists, Nearest-neighbor search, embedding vectors matches, vectors matches faces, missed threat

备注： 14 pages, 12 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Nearest-neighbor search over embedding vectors matches faces against watchlists, transactions against known fraud patterns, and medical images against confirmed cases, where a missed neighbor is a missed threat, fraud, or diagnosis. Because an approximate index cannot report which queries lost a true neighbor, these applications require recall of 0.999 to 0.9999, at a cost far below that of exact search over billions of vectors. Inverted-file indexes reach high recall by probing more lists, but they read and score every probed vector at full precision, so that throughput falls in inverse proportion to the number of probed lists. Graph indexes saturate below these targets, whereas quantized lists must rerank their candidates with the full vectors. Our insight is to decide for almost every probed vector that it cannot enter the top k of the probed lists without reading the vector, through the certified lower bound of exact search by certified exclusion. This paper turns this insight into Lychee, a GPU inverted-file search that screens the probed lists on tensor cores under a threshold from anchors of the same lists and computes exact distances only for the few survivors. We prove that Lychee returns exactly the k nearest vectors of the probed lists, the answer of an inverted-file search at full precision, while it reads a few hundred full vectors per query regardless of the number of probed lists. On eleven datasets from one million to one billion vectors and against ten GPU and CPU baselines, Lychee is 9.7 to 117 times faster than IVF-Flat at equal recall with 200 probed lists on datasets of up to 100 million vectors. It also reaches recall levels that CAGRA and HNSW do not reach and outruns exact search by certified exclusion by 5 to 18 times above ten million vectors. On four GPUs, Lychee searches one billion vectors at recall 0.9999, 44.5 times faster than IVF-Flat on BIGANN-1B.

However, explicit CoT incurs substantial inference overhead by generating lengthy reasoning traces and relies on manually designed templates that struggle to capture diverse, dynamic user interests. 13. 【2610.30664】Compiling Autoregressive LLM Control into Policy-Exact Parallel Inference for Hierarchical RAG

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30664

备注： 5 pages, 2 figures, 1 table

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models increasingly act as controllers in structured decision pipelines, yet their decisions are still executed by autoregressive text decoding. Hierarchical RAG is one such setting: at each frontier of a document tree, an LLM commits to $b$ of $k$ sections under an input-dependent action grammar, executed as either failure-prone free-form JSON or reliable but strictly serial grammar-constrained generation. We show that this serialization is unnecessary for bounded-horizon control: the reachable future forms a shallow action-prefix tree, which we compile --- one root prefill plus one tree-causal probe evaluate every prefix-conditioned action distribution with serial decoding's tokens, positions, and causal ancestry. A prefix-state lemma makes compiled control policy-exact in exact arithmetic: the same policy with a two-forward critical path and zero output tokens. One further probe pass certifies the exact order-marginalized MAP set. Across four document-disjoint splits and two scales on the primary MetaX device, compiled control matches the serial policy on $84$--$89\%$ of questions --- every instrumented divergence a floating-point near-tie --- and cuts control latency by $35$--$47\%$ versus token-wise serial decoding and $6$--$7\%$ versus an idealized chunked engine; an H800 repetition preserves the ordering.

14. 【2610.30612】he Fusion Tax: A Diagnostic Analysis of When Hybrid Retrieval Hurts

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30612

作者：Aaditya Chauhan,Arun Agarwal

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：software documentation surveyed, sparse lexical retriever, surveyed in September, Hybrid retrieval, reciprocal rank fusion

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Hybrid retrieval, which fuses a sparse lexical retriever with a dense neural one, is widely recommended in the software documentation surveyed in September 2026, usually with reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), on the strength of aggregate gains. We ask what fusion costs on individual queries, treating "do not fuse" as a first-class outcome, in the setting in which hybrid retrieval is deployed: a standard Anserini BM25, five dense encoders spanning 22M-596M parameters and three model generations, a learned-sparse (SPLADE) alternative, and a cross-encoder second stage, on seven BEIR datasets and three fusion functions. Even the best method, dense-weighted score interpolation, ranks relevant documents below the better of its two components on 13-48% of queries and below the deployable dense-only retriever on 8-21% (14-48% and 11-25% at a weight selected under leave-one-corpus-out validation), while beating dense-only in aggregate on every corpus with the primary encoder; the downside is significant under loss-averse risk-sensitive evaluation on six of the seven corpora. We call this loss the fusion tax. It is mostly a ranking effect that preserves recall in aggregate, it is most likely on the queries that only one retriever covers, and it persists across the encoder ladder and the SPLADE substitution. RRF carries the higher tax wherever the dense retriever is clearly stronger and only beats dense-only where the two components are balanced. Fusion failure is weakly predictable within a corpus but transfers unreliably across corpora, because the features that predict it change sign with the sparse-dense balance. A cross-encoder recovers part of the tax only where it is itself reliable. We distill these findings into guidance that keys the fusion decision to the measured sparse-dense balance of the target corpus.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30611

作者：Amirhossein Aghaei,Liangyan Li,Shiyi Yang,Shaohua Wang,Yan Wang

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Scaling large language models has substantially improved linguistic fluency, yet reliable autonomous agents require more than fluent generation. In multi-turn inference, current agentic systems often produce semantically plausible but structurally ungrounded reasoning, especially under incomplete observations. We propose Structure-Grounded Uncertainty Reduction (SGUR), a training-free framework that complements token-level generation with active uncertainty reduction over structured hypothesis spaces. SGUR represents incomplete observations as a query graph and aligns them with candidate hypothesis graphs through a prior-weighted partial graph matching mechanism, where database-derived empirical priors assign greater importance to decision-relevant relations rather than treating all graph edges uniformly. The resulting inference state is iteratively refined by an active information-gain strategy that selects the next most informative node to query, systematically pruning the candidate space. We instantiate SGUR on a challenging PrimeKG-derived sequential medical diagnosis benchmark, demonstrating that structural grounding can partially substitute for model scale: starting from only two observed symptoms, SGUR achieves 92.5% Hit@3, outperforming the strongest Graph-RAG baseline by 9.4 percentage points while using approximately 5x fewer tokens. Notably, SGUR enables smaller backbone models to match or exceed the performance of models up to 10x larger, suggesting that structural grounding provides a more robust and efficient paradigm for scaling agent performance than relying exclusively on increasing model size.

17. 【2610.30425】Getting a GRIP on Graph-Based Search for Learned Sparse Retrieval

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30425

作者：Vihan Lakshman,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Databases (cs.DB)

关键词：recently gained traction, LSR, Learned sparse, recently gained, gained traction

备注： 8 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Learned sparse retrieval (LSR) has recently gained traction in the information retrieval community for the ability to combine the generalization capabilities of neural representations with the interpretability of keyword-based search. A number of recent works have demonstrated that inverted indexes, when appropriately modified for learned sparse embeddings, achieve state-of-the-art recall-latency tradeoffs for LSR, often outperforming graph-based indexes by substantial margins in latency at a fixed recall target. As a result, the community has generally concluded that graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search is inferior to inverted indexes for LSR. This paper challenges this conventional wisdom. Our key insight is that, while applying standard graph-based ANN indexes out-of-the-box to sparse vectors leads to uncompetitive performance, we can overcome these shortcomings by infusing the strengths of inverted indexes into graph-based search. In particular, we introduce three techniques to accelerate graph-based LSR: (i) term-based entry point seeding, (ii) approximate routing via sparse sketching, and (iii) sparse partition-based graph construction. We combine these techniques into a new graph-based ANN system for LSR we call GRIP. On the MS MARCO v2 benchmark (138M passages) with SPLADE embeddings, GRIP achieves a $2.5\times$ latency improvement over Seismic, a state-of-the-art inverted index tailored for LSR, at 95\% recall@10. Ultimately, our results call into question the default choice of inverted indexes for deploying learned sparse retrieval.

18. 【2610.30423】When Agentic Retrieval Fails Silently: Framework and Production Evidence from a Creative AI Assistant

点击查看摘要

Abstract:CTR, MRR, and behavioral NDCG assume the user sees ranked results and acts on them. In an agentic assistant, a generation layer consumes retrieval output first, and that assumption breaks in three ways. The agent reads the results before the user does (who). The user's action can come several turns or sessions after retrieval (when). Outcomes blend retrieved content with the model's own knowledge (what caused it). We call this the attribution gap. We propose Layered Agentic Retrieval Assessment (LARA), which measures retrieval quality, engagement and outcome, and product success as three independent layers joined by a four-field lineage schema that requires no new modeling. We examine the motivation on Adobe Express, where one retrieval infrastructure serves a gallery search surface and an agentic assistant at roughly one-eighth its session volume for the same user population. Chat users reformulate more than gallery users (44.4% vs. 33.1%) because chat reformulation refines generated output. LARA restores attribution for the retrieval.

19. 【2610.30365】Data-Void Exploits in Web-Searching LLMs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30365

作者：Michele Guerra,Wuyeh Jobe,Sanjana Nambiar,Christina Pöpper,Azza Abouzied

类目：Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：seek online information, Large Language Models, Web-searching Large Language, read synthesized answers, users seek online

备注： Extended version of the paper to appear in the Network and Distributed System Security (NDSS) Symposium 2027. 23 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Web-searching Large Language Models (LLMs) have changed how users seek online information: instead of sifting through ranked lists of links, users read synthesized answers. This shift creates a new LLM vulnerability: a data-void exploit. Disinformers publish their narratives in search spaces where relevant and reliable content is scarce, i.e. data voids, then rely on the LLM's web-search to retrieve and then use, cite, or list those pages in its responses to data-void seeking prompts. Unlike classical search, where users see ranking cues and competing results, LLMs can obscure when an answer relies on sparse, unverified, or adversarial sources. We study this threat by varying the user's prompt tone - skeptical, excited, or neutral - and LLM choice - ChatGPT, Gemini, or Sonar Pro - as well as three attacker controls. The attacker can use one of five known strategies to construct and fill a data void with their narrative, host their content at varying levels of visibility from well-established, indexed domains to less established ones, and include generative-optimization engine features thought to boost citing by web-searching LLMs. We find that data-void exploits can succeed with only a single attacker controlled page. Across all conditions, attacker-content appears in LLM responses 31% of the time. User controls offer limited protection: while skeptical prompts slightly reduce success and slightly increase flagging or warnings in LLM responses, we find no evidence of large effects of prompt tone or model choice on success rates, although smaller effects cannot be ruled out. Overall, our results establish data voids as a practical inference-time attack surface for manipulating web-searching LLMs through strategically placed web content and highlight the urgent need for defenses against such exploits.

20. 【2610.30359】FAIRGate: Agentic Runtime Policy Selection and Verified Repair for Fairer Recommendation Delivery

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30359

作者：Navkar Jain,Enock Ayiku,Kenneth Fletcher

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Fairness-aware recommender systems, Fairness-aware recommender, fixed re-ranking rule, rule after scoring, recommender systems

备注： Accepted at the 1st IEEE International Conference on Responsible Artificial Intelligence (IRAI 2026), Melbourne, Australia. 6 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Fairness-aware recommender systems often encode one fairness objective during training or apply a fixed re-ranking rule after scoring. In deployment, however, the appropriate fairness action can vary across requests because the candidate pool, item metadata, exposure debt, user preference history, and utility budget all change at runtime. We propose FAIRGate, a model-agnostic delivery controller that places an agentic policy-selection layer between a base ranker and the final top-K list. The agent does not define fairness objectives from scratch. It chooses from a predefined policy library covering long-tail exposure, item-group exposure balance, concentration control, calibration protection, and abstention. A deterministic executor then applies the selected policy through constrained repair, and a validator accepts the result only when relevance, feasibility, metadata, calibration, and audit constraints are satisfied. The final language-model component receives only the verified audit trace and produces evidence-linked narration after the ranking decision is fixed. This design gives the agent runtime responsibility while keeping fairness enforcement measurable, bounded, and replayable.

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems face security, privacy, and copyright risks including content leakage, source reconstruction, unauthorized access, data poisoning, and prompt injection. Yet RAG protection studies use incompatible evaluation metrics, datasets, and threat models. We present a systematic survey and human-coded evidence map of 118 full-text-confirmed RAG defense papers, organized by measured endpoint rather than claimed goal or defense mechanism. Our seven-family taxonomy covers content disclosure, formal privacy proxies, access control and suppression, corpus-use detection, and pipeline integrity and attribution. Thirty-two annotators double-coded the papers kappa=0.867. The survey identifies three gaps. First, retrieval reranking and context rewriting each span five or six endpoint families, so mechanisms do not identify protection outcomes. Second, only 17.8% of studies use content disclosure or reconstruction as their primary endpoint; under the frozen copyright/ownership relation label, 73.3% of records have corpus-use detection as their primary endpoint, and none have an emitted-expression primary endpoint. Third, most evaluations use general-purpose question-answering datasets and non-adaptive attacks rather than sensitive data and adaptive, multi-turn extraction. Our controlled RAG testbed shows that preventing verbatim copying does not prevent private-fact recovery: a normalized 8-gram guard reduces 20-token copying to 0.0%, but a three-query adaptive attacker recovers 48.3% of hidden facts on Qwen3-8B. We release the taxonomy, dataset, coding protocols, and testbed to enable comparable, claim-aligned RAG security evaluation.

Existing compression and elastic representation methods expose smaller configurations, yet making multiple budgets available does not determine which configuration a query should use. The allocation decision must estimate the retrieval value of alternative representations before incurring their full computation, while accommodating different resource preferences. We introduce FlexVDR, a framework for query-adaptive budget allocation within a single retriever. We construct a joint family of routes over encoder depth, embedding width, and token count. 2. 【2610.31670】Sensorimotor Alignment: A Platonic Proxy for Embodied Policy Success

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31670

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Robotics (cs.RO)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Robotic foundation models are typically evaluated through costly closed-loop rollouts, which motivates cheap offline proxies for model capability. The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) suggests that as representations improve, different modalities such as vision and language converge toward a shared statistical model of reality. We ask whether motion trajectories form another such modality: as embodied models become more capable, should their sensory representations converge toward the structure of physical motion? To examine this, we propose Sensorimotor Alignment ($S^2$), a training-free offline metric that measures how well a model's sensory representation aligns with the geometry of physical motion. With $S^2$, we extend PRH to embodied models and make sensorimotor alignment empirically measurable, offering insight into why an embodied model succeeds beyond its closed-loop success rate.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31668

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video foundation models have advanced rapidly in understanding and generating visual sequences, while video world models seek to capture the underlying dynamics of the world they depict. Despite their different objectives, both concern the relationship between video observations and an evolving world. This survey adopts world modeling as a unifying lens for examining these developments. Viewing an observed video as a partial, lossy, and temporally ordered projection of a latent temporal world, we organize the literature around four complementary operations: reading infers world properties from observed video; writing synthesizes video from specified or learned world conditions; sharing integrates understanding and generation through common modeling mechanisms; and interacting predicts and updates world states under actions and evolving observations. This perspective distinguishes temporal coherence in observed or generated sequences from consistency in the underlying world state. Across tasks, representations, models, datasets, and benchmarks, we trace a shift in emphasis from clip-level recognition and perceptual fidelity toward persistent state, controllable evolution, physical and causal validity, and evaluation grounded in interaction and downstream decisions, and we distill the open challenges that follow.

7. 【2610.31645】OmniRay: Camera-Agnostic Monocular 3D Reconstruction with Composite Ray Fields

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31645

作者：Namitha Guruprasad,Abhay Yadav,Cheng Peng,Rama Chellappa,Anand Bhattad

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://nam1410.github.io/omniray/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Monocular models recover 3D geometry from a single image in one pass, but most assume a pinhole camera and produce distorted geometry on the wide-angle imagery produced by fisheye and panoramic lenses. Camera-aware models remove this assumption by predicting a per-pixel ray field. However, they represent different camera geometries using a single fixed basis to capture both the overall camera projection and its finer variations. We argue that camera geometry can be viewed as having a hierarchy of complexity for representing rays. We present OmniRay, a feed-forward model whose Ray Module is composed of three components: a monotone radial base that captures radially symmetric structure, a spherical-harmonic residual for smooth deviations from it, and a free-form residual for remaining local variation. These components are composed as successive rotations on the sphere, allowing the representation to accommodate diverse camera geometries. Given a single image, OmniRay predicts a ray direction and an up-to-scale radial distance for every pixel, from which a 3D point cloud is recovered without needing camera information at inference time. Extensive experiments across different camera configurations demonstrate that OmniRay establishes a new state-of-the-art in camera-agnostic monocular 3D reconstruction.

9. 【2610.31641】UniVOE: A Removal-Driven Framework for Interaction-Aware Universal Video Object Editing

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video object removal and injection are reciprocal editing operations between object-present and object-absent video states, yet they differ substantially in training difficulty. Removal can be effectively bootstrapped from video inpainting and further adapted to erase object-related interaction effects. Injection, however, requires large-scale paired supervision to model object appearance, dynamics, and scene-dependent effects, while perfectly aligned real-world pairs are difficult to collect and synthetic data lacks sufficient realism and diversity. To address this challenge, we present UniVOE, a removal-driven framework for training both video object removal and injection models. We first train an effect-aware remover by transferring video inpainting priors and finetuning on object-present/object-absent pairs. The trained remover converts real object-present videos into object-absent counterparts, creating large-scale paired supervision for injection. An injection model is trained on these pairs in the reverse direction to recover objects together with their dynamics and interaction effects. Moreover, since pixel-level reconstruction alone cannot adequately capture perceptual realism, we introduce a reward-based post-training method that aligns injection results with human preferences using a proprietary reward model. Experiments against strong open-source and commercial systems demonstrate that UniVOE achieves state-of-the-art performance on both object removal and injection, particularly in challenging interaction-effect cases.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31640

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video diffusion models have learned rich 3D/4D priors, making them promising for scene generation and video re-rendering. However, their use in spatial tasks is limited by imprecise camera control, spatial and temporal inconsistencies, and entangled camera motion with scene dynamics. Existing approaches, such as task-specific fine-tuning and post-process warping, often introduce visual artifacts, generalize poorly, or incur high computational costs. We propose WorldForge V2 to achieve accurate camera control while preserving the generative capabilities of pretrained video models. We first develop a training-free guidance framework using warped references to iteratively refine generation along a target camera path. Optical-flow analysis identifies motion-related latent channels for selective guidance, helping separate motion control from appearance. A dual-path strategy further compares guided and unguided predictions to correct artifacts from imperfect references. Together, these components guide viewpoint changes while preserving scene appearance and object motion. To reduce refinement costs, v-cache approximates the correction using the preceding denoising prediction, avoiding an additional model evaluation. We further extend motion localization from latent channels to network blocks and introduce Trajectory LoRA, which concentrates adaptation on blocks most responsive to trajectory guidance. A two-stage training strategy teaches these adapters to denoise the states encountered during guided generation, using limited supervision effectively. This enables reliable camera control with very little training data, with a single training scene already supporting generalization to unseen scenes. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations across multiple datasets show that our method achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and trajectory accuracy in camera-controlled video generation.

11. 【2610.31635】HiP3R: Hierarchical Pose Graphs for Long-Horizon 3D Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31635

作者：Zunnan Xu,Shangchen Zhou,Yihang Luo,Chen Change Loy

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Feed-forward models accurately, Feed-forward models, models accurately estimate, geometry from short, models accurately

备注： Technical Report

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feed-forward models accurately estimate camera motion and 3D geometry from short video windows. However, computational and memory costs prevent direct inference on sequences containing thousands of frames. Long videos must therefore be processed as overlapping chunks. This division produces independent local reconstructions with inconsistent coordinate frames and duplicate estimates of shared cameras. Achieving global consistency requires propagating constraints from adjacent overlaps and distant revisits across the sequence. This requirement naturally motivates pose graph optimization. However, the choice of graph level creates a trade-off. Chunk-level graphs efficiently resolve scale drift and close large loops. Yet, their rigid transformations cannot correct internal errors or conflicting frame predictions. Conversely, frame-level optimization offers local flexibility, but scaling it globally is computationally expensive and can break metric scale. HiP3R resolves this trade-off using a hierarchical pose graph. HiP3R first optimizes a robust chunk-level $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ graph to establish the global layout and scale. It then holds this layout fixed and estimates one shared $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ camera per frame, turning overlapping window predictions into a single trajectory. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of HiP3R in improving camera trajectories and 3D reconstruction. Our method applies to both chunk-based and streaming backends without additional training or fine-tuning. The codes are available at this https URL.

12. 【2610.31623】F2G-Pose: Geometry-Aware Foundation Feature Lifting for Direct RGB-D Category-Level Object Pose Estimation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31623

作者：Jinyu Zhang,Haitao Lin,Jiashu Hou,Xiangyang Xue,Yanwei Fu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：instance-specific CAD models, object pose estimation, paper studies category-level, RGB-D object pose, category-level RGB-D object

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 22 pages, including the complete technical appendix

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This paper studies category-level RGB-D object pose estimation, which recovers an object's 3D rotation and translation without instance-specific CAD models or reference views. This task is highly ambiguous due to intra-class variations, symmetries, and partial observations. Current solutions struggle to balance reliability and efficiency: methods based on intermediate structures are prone to error propagation, while diffusion models suffer from high inference latency and complex post-hoc candidate extraction. To address this, we present F2G-Pose, a real-time direct, single-pass framework that predicts category-level pose, metric size, and camera-frame completed shape from a single RGB-D observation in one feed-forward pass. F2G-Pose lifts dense visual foundation features to partial point clouds, encodes local geometry with DGCNN tokenization, and integrates object-level structure with a Mixture-of-Experts Transformer. Joint size prediction and camera-frame shape completion encourage metric consistency through dense geometric supervision. With its task-specific network trained only on pose-annotated synthetic SOPE data and its frozen RADIO backbone externally pretrained, F2G-Pose achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SOPE and ROPE while running at 28 FPS for pose-only inference, and further demonstrates strong camera-frame geometry on HouseCat6D and real-object transfer on HANDAL. Analyses of latency, robustness, shape completion, and GenPose++ candidate behavior further demonstrate the efficiency and reliability of F2G-Pose, with real-world manipulation experiments demonstrating deployment potential under segmented RGB-D inputs. The project page, code, and pretrained models are made available at this https URL.

13. 【2610.31592】Beyond Best-of-$k$: Prior-Consistent DAPS for Ambiguous Nonlinear Inverse Problems

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31592

作者：Merve Kocabas,Benjamin A. Burns,Andreas Geiger,Kent Gauen,Sara Fridovich-Keil

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Diffusion models, solving inverse problems, inverse problems, nonlinear inverse problems, solving inverse

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion models have become strong priors for solving inverse problems. However, nonlinear inverse problems such as phase retrieval remain challenging when multiple measurement-consistent solutions are distinguishable only by the prior; we call such measurements ambiguous. Existing diffusion-based samplers rely on local approximations during reconstruction. For an ambiguous measurement, these approximations can allow measurement guidance to favor a measurement-consistent mode that is close to a prior-supported mode but is itself not supported by the prior. Evaluation often masks this type of failure by reporting the best-of-$k$ runs, where the best is selected by comparison to the ground truth. We introduce Prior-Consistent DAPS (PC-DAPS), a variant of Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling (DAPS) designed to address this failure case by maintaining multiple candidate solutions within a single sampling run. As annealing noise decreases, PC-DAPS prunes and replaces weaker candidates according to their prior consistency, without any ground-truth access. PC-DAPS reduces the incidence of prior-inconsistent samples and improves sample quality on nonlinear inverse problems while preserving sample quality and diversity on linear inverse problems.

14. 【2610.31566】CellTrack-Bench: Microscopic Cell Tracking Benchmark for Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31566

作者：Ilan Theodoro,Jordão Bragantini,Loïc A. Royer

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Large language models, Large language, shown outstanding performance, shown outstanding, text-first tasks

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models have shown outstanding performance on text-first tasks such as coding, math, and writing. Recent efforts are shifting models' training to include scientific problems that often live in highly specialized domains beyond text, such as protein structure and biological images. However, in these scenarios, their general-purpose accuracy compared with specialized algorithms remains in question. One such domain is cell tracking, which requires spatiotemporal awareness of cell dynamics. To evaluate this, we created a new benchmark, CellTrack-Bench, which assesses Vision-Language Models' (VLMs) cell-tracking-related performance against specialized trackers. The full benchmark draws from 15 standard cell-tracking datasets across four imaging modalities and comprises 1,537 problems corresponding to 9,041 cell-linking decisions. We selected a hard subset requiring greater reasoning effort by identifying failures shared across distinct algorithmic trackers. With this proposed dataset, we: (i) evaluated different environment harnesses for VLMs; (ii) benchmarked 9 frontier closed-source models and 13 open-weight models; and (iii) provided a detailed analysis of their failure modes. Code, the stored model outputs, and a pipeline that regenerates the benchmark from the source datasets are available at this https URL.

15. 【2610.31565】Squaring-the-Sphere: Distortion-Free and Seamless Panorama Generation via CubeAtlas

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31565

作者：Xuhui Zuo,Chenjie Cao,Tengfei Wang,Junta Wu,Yisu Zhang,Chunchao Guo

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：global spherical continuity, requires both local, global spherical, local perspective fidelity, Generating

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generating 360-degree panoramas requires both local perspective fidelity and global spherical continuity. Existing representations face a trade-off: equirectangular projection (ERP) enables seamless panorama modeling but suffers from severe distortion and a domain gap to pre-trained image generators, whereas cubemaps preserve perspective geometry but often produce inconsistent faces and visible seams. We present Squaring-the-Sphere, a panoramic image generation framework built on a novel representation CubeAtlas, which unfolds all cubemap faces into a single compact canvas. By arranging orientation-aligned, rotated polar regions alongside the equatorial faces, this representation preserves the perspective geometry of cubemaps while organizing the cross-face relationships required for seamless panorama assembly. It further enables a pretrained diffusion transformer to model the complete panorama jointly in a single pass, without altering the backbone architecture or positional encoding. While CubeAtlas has a token count comparable to ERP, its structured layout makes many dense attention interactions unnecessary. We therefore introduce a layout-aware sparse attention mask that preserves the interactions required for panorama continuity while removing geometrically irrelevant token pairs. As a result, CubeAtlas achieves better quality than ERP modeling with lower attention computation. Furthermore, we introduce a training-free super-resolution technique and a fusion scheme that enhance fine details in distortion-free perspective space before achieving the final ERP panorama. Experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves geometric fidelity, cross-view consistency, and high-resolution visual quality over previous methods.

16. 【2610.31538】LACToS: Local Anomaly Detection via Conditional Typicality of Superpixels

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31538

作者：Jihoon Hong,Alireza Kheirandish,Rohan Sanda,Sara Fridovich-Keil

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：identifying spatially confined, spatially confined anomalous, task of identifying, identifying spatially, spatially confined

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Local anomaly detection is the task of identifying spatially confined anomalous or out-of-distribution (OOD) regions within an otherwise in-distribution (ID) image. Most existing methods are designed for a specific context, with a strong focus on industrial defect detection, where they achieve excellent performance on well-established benchmarks. However, we observe that these methods can exhibit significant performance drops in other contexts, especially when the inlier distribution is multimodal. To account for this inlier complexity, we propose LACToS, a principled local OOD detection method based on statistical typicality and made tractable through two innovations: (i) grouping pixels into superpixels and (ii) using a computationally efficient proxy to estimate typicality. We evaluate LACToS across three diverse applications spanning medical imaging, human faces, and X-ray security screening. LACToS performs more robustly than prior methods across these benchmarks. The code is released here: this https URL.

17. 【2610.31523】Diffusion-Based Synthesis of Complete 3D Biparametric Prostate MRI

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31523

作者：Claudia Giardina,Montse Pardàs,Verónica Vilaplana

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：representative prostate MRI, Synthetic biparametric MRI, deep learning applications, prostate MRI datasets, biparametric MRI

备注： Presented at the 6th Deep Generative Models Workshop (DGM4MICCAI), MICCAI 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Synthetic biparametric MRI (bpMRI) may help alleviate the limited availability of large and representative prostate MRI datasets for deep learning applications. In this work, we present a conditional 3D diffusion framework for anatomy-conditioned synthesis of complete prostate bpMRI from anatomical masks. The framework supports sequential synthesis, in which T2w and diffusion modalities are generated by separately trained models, and joint synthesis, in which all bpMRI modalities are generated simultaneously within a single model. Five configurations were trained using PI-CAI, FastMRI Prostate, and PROSTATEx datasets to investigate different conditioning schemes, target modalities, and synthesis strategies. Evaluation using distribution-based metrics, paired-image similarity, and qualitative assessment demonstrated the feasibility of both synthesis pathways. Joint synthesis achieved competitive distributional fidelity while generating complete bpMRI, whereas direct synthesis of ADC and computed high-b-value DWI outperformed their derivation from synthetic diffusion-weighted images.

18. 【2610.31505】Evaluation of Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Software Ensembles for Hippocampal Segmentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31505

作者：Gabriel Oliveira-Stahl,Anna Schroder,James Moggridge,Hamza A. Salhab,Caroline Micallef,Josephine Barnes,M. Jorge Cardoso,Carole H. Sudre,Matthew Grech-Sollars

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：monitoring neurological conditions, tool for diagnosing, diagnosing and monitoring, monitoring neurological, neurological conditions

备注： Accepted for publication at the Journal of Machine Learning for Biomedical Imaging (MELBA) [this https URL](https://melba-journal.org/2026:030)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Accurate hippocampal segmentation can be a useful tool for diagnosing and monitoring neurological conditions such as Alzheimer's disease and epilepsy. While numerous automated segmentation methods exist, their clinical adoption remains limited. Reliable uncertainty assessment can enhance trust and facilitate clinical translation. This study evaluates ensembles of five heterogeneous hippocampal segmentation methods - InnerEye, ASHS, FastSurfer, HippoSeg, and FreeSurfer - across two dementia datasets and one epilepsy dataset. The sub-ensemble containing InnerEye, FastSurfer, and HippoSeg emerged as both accurate and efficient, highlighting the feasibility of balancing computational cost and performance. Additionally, ensemble-derived uncertainty quantification with sample variance, mutual information, and predictive entropy is shown to reduce inaccurate segmentations by flagging low-confidence cases, potentially providing a mechanism for automatically escalating ambiguous cases for expert assessment. Lastly, this study explores the downstream clinical utility of ensemble-derived uncertainty, finding a significant correlation with motion artefact severity and a modest contribution to Alzheimer's disease classification.

19. 【2610.31494】AgentVQA: A Unified Benchmark for Agentic Visual Understanding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31494

作者：Advait Gupta,Sachin Konan,Tom Yin,Gabriel Sarch,Zhuang Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：remains poorly understood, contexts remains poorly, poorly understood, agentic contexts remains, Vision-language models

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://zlab-princeton.github.io/AgentVQA)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language models (VLMs) can perform a broad range of tasks across diverse settings, yet their performance in agentic contexts remains poorly understood. Although online evaluations in simulators and real-world environments are essential for measuring interactive agent performance, they are costly and challenging to scale. Complementary offline VQA benchmarks can enable faster and more reliable comparisons across agentic domains, yet existing benchmarks remain limited and fragmented. To address this gap, we introduce AgentVQA, an offline benchmark for evaluating VLMs on realistic agentic scenarios across five agent domains: Web Agents, Robotics, Egocentric Videos, Games, and Spatial Planning. We build AgentVQA by manually selecting 1000 challenging, real-world agent examples from 23 source datasets and adapting them for reliable offline evaluation. The resulting benchmark preserves realistic scenarios from interactive environments across diverse domains, and its model rankings track those from corresponding online evaluations. Across the 22 evaluated models, the best achieves only 58.5% accuracy. Our cross-domain analysis shows that errors are driven primarily by failures to ground and maintain accurate representations of the current and evolving visual state, including localizing relevant UI elements, interpreting motion, tracking state changes over time, and inferring 3D spatial relationships, rather than by task misinterpretation or downstream logical reasoning.

20. 【2610.31478】MoG-VLM: Efficient Video Anomaly Detection through Motion-Guided Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31478

作者：Khaled Waleed Dawoud,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer,Khan Muhammad,Muhammad Haris Khan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：shown strong potential, Vision-Language Models, frame-level inference makes, real-time deployment, video anomaly detection

备注： Accepted at BMVC 2026. Includes supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for video anomaly detection, but dense frame-level inference makes them impractical for real-time deployment. Existing acceleration commonly relies on uniform sampling, which ignores content dynamics and can either waste computation on static segments or miss localized anomalies. We propose MoG-VLM a motion-adaptive anomaly detection framework that leverages motion purely as a lightweight control signal to decide when VLM inference is necessary. To this end, we introduce Temporal Gradient Magnitude (TGM) which performs content-aware sampling to concentrate computation on informative regions while avoiding redundant processing. We further introduce a hierarchical dual-stream design in which dense multi-frame VLM reasoning is activated only around frames with elevated anomaly likelihood, via anchor-triggered context-window expansion with early termination. A fusion module then reconciles these instantaneous semantic cues with temporal action dynamics. Across four benchmarks, our method achieves 91.91%, 84.30%, 87.88%, and 78.96% AUC-ROC on XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, MSAD, and ShanghaiTech, respectively, establishing new state of the art on three benchmarks under the zero-shot, training-free setting. On XD-Violence, we improve over the previous best result by +0.57% while delivering a 1.31x inference speedup. These results demonstrate that content-aware allocation of VLM capacity, processing fewer frames more intelligently rather than all frames uniformly, is a more effective axis of progress than uniformly densifying VLM inference across frames. Code is available on GitHub: this https URL.

21. 【2610.31471】Ask About the Right Moment: Training-Free Object Identity Reasoning for Video-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31471

作者：Di Yang,Xiaochang Li,Muyang Liu,Yufei Wang,Yanhai Xiong

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：concern one physical, model, physical object, video concern, video

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Many questions about a video concern one physical object. Where does the ball end up after the cups are shuffled? Which card is the queen? How often was the mug covered? Video-language models (Video-LMs) recognise such objects in single frames but do not keep them apart over time. On real shell-game videos, open models from 8B to 235B parameters, their thinking variants, a reinforcement-learned tool agent and three training-free tool agents all stay within ten points of chance. We separate semantic identity binding from temporal identity maintenance. An object's identity should be fixed at a moment when the evidence for it is visible, and carried afterwards by a tracker. \emph{Track, Then Bind} implements this principle without training. A promptable video tracker follows every candidate object and draws it with a track number. A frozen vision-language model answers a static question about the identifying evidence, which binds the target to a track. The answer is then derived from the tracked state in text, without asking the model to reinterpret the original video during read-out. A single configuration covers all benchmarks. With Qwen3-VL-32B it solves both VET-Bench tasks, where a fine-tuned trajectory model reaches 92\% and 90\% and the same VLM voting over 16 samples stays at 30--32\%. It answers 55.4\% of the hardest Perception Test cup games, where the best published model answers 41.5\%, and, using track-derived timelines, 48.6\% of TOC-Bench questions, against 37.1\% for the same model answering directly. Drawing the same tracks on the video and asking the question directly falls 12--19 points short of the agent on real cup games.

22. 【2610.31466】AnyGameRenderer: Entity-Consistent Generative Rendering Across Games and an Entity-Level Benchmark

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31466

作者：Ruicong Liu,Liangyang Ouyang,Fengbo Lan,Zhixiang Wang,Kaipeng Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Toggle, flat semantic blobs, build small playable, small playable games, Toggle Hugging Face

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI coding agents can now build small playable games in minutes, but the results usually look like programmer art: gray blockouts, colored geometric primitives, and flat semantic blobs. We present AnyGameRenderer, a video-to-video generative renderer that turns such footage into high-quality visuals with characters, motion detail, and effects. Throughout, it keeps the layout, camera, and every entity's position and timing intact. Our AnyGameRenderer generalizes across a wide range of genres that agents build, such as tower defense, auto-battlers, top-down survivors, turn-based battles, kart racing, and crowd runners. AnyGameRenderer takes plain video as its game-state input, with no complex representations such as engine G-buffers, per-object class labels, or low-poly proxies. Gray blockouts, colored primitives, semantic maps, and wireframes all work as input. It also preserves entity correspondence: group counts stay correct, and entities appear and disappear on the right frame. We implement AnyGameRenderer by LoRA fine-tuning MiniMax-H3 ref2va, a 33B reference-video-conditioned video generation model. Training uses $\sim$10k paired clips from three sources: (i) multi-stream rendering data from 38 agent-made games exported from GEAR, an AI game-creation tool; (ii) two-stream rendering data from Unreal Engine, with programmatically controlled crowds, count changes, and entity scales; and (iii) real gameplay recordings, with pairs reconstructed via 3D-box detection. To evaluate the entity correspondence, we propose an entity-level benchmark of 59 held-out cases with 753 entities, scored against ground-truth boxes. Compared with the base model, AnyGameRenderer better preserves entity correspondence: temporal match rises from 50.9\% to 62.9\%, missed count changes fall from 34.7\% to 21.3\%, and fewer entities are hallucinated or left unrendered.

23. 【2610.31455】MixPT: Point Transformer with Interleaved Window and Global Attention

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31455

作者：Minkyun Seo,Hyungtae Lim,Jaesik Park

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：capture detailed local, detailed local geometry, global attention, attention, cloud transformers aim

备注： 23 pages, 14 figures, 12 tables. Project page: [this https URL](https://minkyunseo.github.io/MixPT/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Point cloud transformers aim to capture detailed local geometry and relationships across a scene at a manageable computational cost. Two common approaches are window attention and global attention. They differ in how they enable scene-wide interaction, which refers to information exchange among tokens across a scene. Window attention reduces computational cost by restricting self-attention to groups of tokens called windows; in serialized designs, each window contains a fixed number of consecutive tokens from a spatially ordered sequence. Because each block only directly connects tokens within the same window, information exchange across windows relies on changes in window membership across blocks and hierarchical pooling. Global attention, in contrast, directly connects all tokens within a scene, but computing attention over all token pairs can be costly when the number of tokens is large. To balance computational cost and scene-wide interaction, we introduce MixPT, a point transformer that uses a mix of window and global attention blocks in an interleaved schedule, while retaining a single fixed serialization pattern for its window blocks. For scenes with a large number of tokens, MixPT applies global attention to spatially pooled tokens and returns residual updates to the original-resolution stream. Across indoor and outdoor benchmarks, MixPT achieves competitive accuracy with lower latency than representative window and global attention baselines. We further demonstrate the feasibility of scaling the attention stage in depth by repeating the interleaved pattern of window and global attention blocks.

24. 【2610.31432】A Sparse Topometric Graph for Scalable Cross-Session Indoor Navigation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31432

作者：Eun Sun Lee,Minkyun Seo,Gwangtak Bae,Junho Kim,Seunggu Kang,Jaesik Park,Young Min Kim

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：conventional embodied agent, indoor environment builds, accumulating noisy real-world, noisy real-world measurements, conventional embodied

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A conventional embodied agent in an indoor environment builds a map by accumulating noisy real-world measurements and results from heavy inference to guide navigation. While the detailed measurements may differ due to scene changes or sensor settings, the unique topological structure of navigable passages or the connectivity between rooms may provide training-free guidance that complements local observations. We propose a sparse topometric graph as an efficient structural representation for robust indoor navigation, alleviating the need for fine-grained matching, which is computationally expensive, memory-intensive, and prone to real-world variations. The map consists of nodes that encode local 3D geometry and distance-weighted edges that capture traversable connectivity. A learned graph-matching network finds consistent subgraph structures across sessions by considering both node-level geometric descriptors and neighborhood connectivity. The holistic contextual matching is robust to local ambiguities caused by weak distinctiveness or repetitive structure, and establishes stable correspondences despite local scene changes, different sensor modalities, or limited spatial overlap. We analyze the practicality of the graph through cross-session point-goal navigation, where a goal specified in a prior reference map must be reached from an unknown initial pose in a later session. Experiments show that our method achieves the highest success rates (77.1% on Gibson and 40.0% under 3D-FRONT scene changes), while reducing memory by more than two orders of magnitude compared to point-level representations. The proposed approach further supports cross-session alignment and goal-directed navigation with strong scalability and robust real-world transfer.

25. 【2610.31385】A Task-Centric Survey of Long-Document Visual Question Answering: Taxonomy, Evolution, and Future Directions

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31385

作者：Yufei Tong,Guanjie Cheng,Tianle Wu,Leilei Zhang,Hang Zheng,Peihan Wu,Jianwei Yin,Shuiguang Deng

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：growing research interest, attracting growing research, visually rich documents, essential for acquiring, acquiring knowledge

备注： 26 pages, 9 figures, 6 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual document understanding is essential for acquiring knowledge from visually rich documents and is attracting growing research interest. Existing surveys emphasize architectures or technical approaches, with limited attention to how task bottlenecks shape technical choices and research priorities. We present a task-centric survey that decomposes long-document visual question answering (VQA) into four components: document parsing, evidence organization and retrieval, generator organization and learning, and evaluation. For each, we develop a fine-grained taxonomy and trace motivations, technical responses, and cross-component connections. We focus on explicit retrieval while covering direct reading. Our temporal analysis traces expanding priorities from multimodal content understanding to sufficient, high-fidelity evidence acquisition and sustained evidence management. We outline future directions in evidence sufficiency, verifiable and correctable understanding, and budget-aware coordination, with an outlook on continual document understanding.

26. 【2610.31329】WeVisOne-VL: Unified Visual Perception from Objects to Pixels

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31329

作者：Tianming Liang,Junyi Pan,Yuxi Li,Canmiao Fu,Zhipeng Huang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Existing vision-language models, Existing vision-language, remain unreliable, unreliable for fine-grained, Existing

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Existing vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable for fine-grained visual perception. In this report, we present WeVisOne-VL (WeChat Vision All-in-One), a powerful VLM that unifies object-level and pixel-level perception through structured next-token prediction. It supports multiple visual tasks including object detection, referring localization, counting, OCR, segmentation, and matting, enabling localization at any user-specific granularity, from points and boxes to masks and alpha mattes. Architecturally, we integrate a lightweight pixel decoder into the VLM via a special OBJ token to generate instance-level pixel outputs. We train the model with a three-stage curriculum SFT followed by mixed-task RL. Across over 30 benchmarks, WeVisOne-VL demonstrates leading performance on both object-level and pixel-level tasks, while maintaining robust rejection for object-absent queries. By releasing WeVisOne-VL to the open-source community, we hope to advance VLMs from broad visual understanding toward truly reliable perception systems for real-world deployment.

27. 【2610.31321】MVLoMa: Multi-View Local Feature Matching with Any Geometry Foundation Model

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31321

作者：Xinyang Lu,Shengzhe Wei,Sheng Tang,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：visual localization commonly, localization commonly match, Local feature matching, feature matching, matching is typically

备注： Project Page: [this https URL](https://kosmoresearch.github.io/MVLoMa/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Local feature matching is typically formulated for image pairs, although structure-from-motion and visual localization commonly match one reference image against many retrieved or covisible neighbours. Applying a pairwise matcher independently to these centre-neighbour edges duplicates reference-side computation and prevents the matcher from exploiting complementary multi-view geometric evidence from the remaining observations. To address these limitations, we introduce MVLoMa, a sparse star-graph matcher that extends LoMa from image pairs to multiple views. MVLoMa first uses a frozen multi-view geometry model to jointly describe all images in a star, then injects the resulting cross-view geometric context into local descriptors. It then matches the centre image to all neighbours in a single forward pass through per-view self-attention and bidirectional centre-neighbour cross-attention. For supervision, we derive positive correspondences only from depth-valid, mutually visible dense warps and explicitly penalize high-confidence matches that violate epipolar geometry. On 25-view star graphs from HyperSim, ScanNet++, Mip-NeRF 360, and Tanks and Temples, MVLoMa obtains the best macro-average relative-pose AUC among the compared methods at all three thresholds (58.65/85.41/90.99). Its matcher is 2.20 times faster than sequential evaluation of the 24 centre-neighbour edges. The same design supports four tested frozen geometry encoders -- DA3, $\pi^3$, VGGT, and VGGT-$\Omega$ -- with DA3-Giant performing best on all reported encoder-ablation metrics. Controlled ablations further isolate the effects of geometry-aware descriptors, visibility filtering, geometric negative supervision, and joint multi-view inference.

28. 【2610.31320】Stream4D: Streaming 4D Reconstruction with Training-free Motion Decoupling via Causal KV-Cache Attention

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31320

作者：Wei Zhang,Ying Feng,Mengtong Wu,Shiqiang Gong,Huaqi Zhang,Qi Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： 10 pages, 8 figures. Accepted to ACM Multimedia 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:4D scene reconstruction requires robust handling of dynamic objects, yet existing streaming methods commonly assume static scenes, leading to pose drift and ghosting artifacts over long sequences. We introduce Stream4D, a training-free, motion-decoupled streaming reconstruction framework that can be directly integrated into causal Transformer backbones (e.g., StreamVGGT, Stream3R). Our key observation is that causal attention implicitly encodes rich temporal motion cues within the KV cache. We adaptively aggregate these cues from multiple attention layers and across temporal windows, and fuse them with depth priors to handle challenging cases such as occlusions and textureless regions, producing precise dynamic masks that decouple static and dynamic regions. We then apply a soft key masking scheme that leverages these dynamic masks to suppress interference from dynamic regions in causal attention, thereby improving camera pose estimation accuracy. Stream4D inherits the efficiency of causal attention and remains compatible with existing streaming architectures. On diverse dynamic-scene benchmarks, Stream4D significantly improves camera pose estimation and static-scene reconstruction quality, yields reliable dynamic masks, and effectively mitigates long-sequence ghosting.

29. 【2610.31304】Data-Driven Vision-Language Models for Remote Sensing: A Survey

Abstract:Online semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables open-vocabulary scene understanding from streaming observations. However, existing methods recover geometric context missing from image-derived semantic features using an additionally learned 3D network, incurring extra training and runtime overhead. We observe that such geometric-semantic evidence may already exist in the agent's observation history and can be retrieved rather than learned. 32. 【2610.31284】Retrieval-Augmented Online Textual Memory for Episodic Memory Video Question Answering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31284

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long egocentric videos pose a central challenge for multimodal language models: the visual stream must be compressed into a memory representation that can be searched efficiently while still preserving the evidence needed for question answering. Building on an online textual-memory framework in which first-person videos are converted into temporally grounded captions, this work systematically studies the role of retrieval in episodic-memory video question answering. We evaluate how different retrieval units, query formulations, context budgets, and reranking strategies affect multiple-choice answering on QAEgo4D-Closed, isolating retrieval as the main variable while keeping the underlying generated memory fixed. More importantly, our analysis shows that better temporal coverage does not necessarily translate into better answers: increasing the retrieval budget improves temporal Hit@K and Recall@K, but can reduce accuracy by introducing visually plausible distractors and uncertain caption evidence. These findings suggest that retrieval-augmented egocentric memory should not be optimized only for evidence recall, but also for the reliability, specificity, and answer-discriminative value of the retrieved context. The study motivates more structured episodic memories that move beyond raw caption retrieval toward evidence-aware representations for long-form egocentric reasoning.

33. 【2610.31282】ReCoG: Relational Concept Graph for Training-Free Personalization

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31282

作者：Changseon Yu,Hyeokjun Kweon

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Training-free personalization adapts, frozen vision-language model, recognize user-defined concepts, retrieving concept entries, Training-free personalization

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Training-free personalization adapts a frozen vision-language model (VLM) to recognize user-defined concepts from only a few exemplars by retrieving concept entries at inference time. While recent retriever-based methods enrich each concept with descriptive evidence, they largely treat concepts in isolation, so the retrieved information can be plausible yet non-discriminative, especially when the personalized concept set contains highly confusable, fine-grained categories. We argue that personalization is inherently relational: correct recognition depends on the specific distinctions between a concept and its nearest alternatives. To this end, we propose ReCoG, a Relational Concept Graph for retrieval-augmented personalization. ReCoG represents concepts as nodes and augments them with directed edges that explicitly capture discriminative cues between concept pairs, elicited by a frozen VLM during database construction. At inference, ReCoG retrieves a shortlist and resolves ambiguity via explicit pairwise comparisons conditioned on the corresponding relational edges, selecting the concept that consistently wins against competing candidates. We also introduce FINGER benchmark to test personalized recognition on confusable concept sets. Across FINGER, ReCoG substantially outperforms prior methods, with the largest gains in the most confusable regimes. ReCoG also achieves consistently best performance on established personalization benchmarks including captioning and VQA, confirming that relational evidence is broadly beneficial beyond fine-grained settings. Abstract:Limited tumour visibility on intra-operative images complicates surgical navigation, including liver hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) targeting on computed tomography (CT) imaging. 35. 【2610.31228】Lightweight Frame Compensation for Real-Time Polyp Segmentation under Frame Skipping

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31228

作者：Weihao Gao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：strict latency budgets, deploying high-accuracy models, latency budgets, computer-assisted colonoscopy, colonoscopy is hampered

备注： 15 pages, 1 figure

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Real-time polyp segmentation in computer-assisted colonoscopy is hampered by the difficulty of deploying high-accuracy models under strict latency budgets. Frame skipping, which performs inference every K frames and reuses or interpolates the rest, improves throughput but introduces motion blur and lesion misalignment that degrade temporal consistency. To close this gap, we propose the Lightweight Frame Compensator (LFC), a 77K-parameter plug-and-play module inserted before a frozen segmentation backbone. Operating on a three-frame window, LFC fuses consecutive frames via spatio-temporal attention into a single compensated frame, and is trained only on pseudo-labels generated by a differently seeded model, without any expert annotation. Experiments on three polyp video benchmarks show that LFC recovers most of the accuracy degraded by direct frame skipping and, in several settings, reaches the accuracy of frame-by-frame inference, while retaining a 2.05X--2.55X throughput gain. We further evaluate the temporal stability of three backbones spanning CNN, pyramid Transformer, and global-attention Transformer architectures, revealing a spectrum of sensitivity to frame skipping that arises from how locally or globally each architecture aggregates misaligned input. The compensation gain of LFC follows this spectrum: it is largest for high-capacity Transformer backbones, which achieve the best static accuracy but suffer the most under skip-frame inference. LFC thus offers a lightweight, pluggable, and deployment-friendly route to real-time polyp segmentation.

Abstract:Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) aims to recover well-exposed and perceptually faithful images from severely degraded observations. Recent diffusion-based generative methods achieve strong perceptual quality by leveraging powerful image priors, but their iterative sampling incurs substantial computational cost, limiting practical deployment. Despite recent efficiency gains in generative LLIE, high-quality enhancement with a single function evaluation and low overall computation remains challenging, particularly under diverse real-world low-light conditions. These findings highlight the potential of GLOW-FM to bring generative LLIE closer to practical deployment through single-step inference and compact latent modeling. 37. 【2610.31209】Moving Regions, Not Moving Objects: Segmenting What Actually Moves under Camera Motion

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31209

作者：Wenhua Jin,Tiancheng Chen,Sheng Tang,Jiahao Li,Yupeng Liu,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Moving object segmentation, object level, conventionally formulated, object segmentation, Moving object

备注： Project page and video demonstrations: [this https URL](https://kosmoresearch.github.io/MoRSe/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Moving object segmentation is conventionally formulated at the sequence and object level, marking an entire instance as moving once it moves anywhere in a video. Recent instantaneous formulations distinguish moving from stationary intervals, but still generate masks at the object level through frozen segmentation foundation models. Because the mask is derived from an instance-level proposal, motion is typically attributed to the entire instance rather than localized to the regions that actually move. Moreover, such instance-level predictions can produce masks even when no part of the instance is actually moving, revealing a more fundamental issue: motion is not an intrinsic property of an instance; it can only be determined by comparing at least two frames. Building on this premise, we introduce MoRSe, which jointly interprets two frames to predict a moving-region mask, distinguishing actual scene motion from apparent motion induced by the camera. A single RGB encoder replaces multi-stage foundation stacks, so no optical flow, point tracks, or separate mask generator is needed at inference. We release MoRSe-5K, a benchmark of 42 sequences with Static, Dynamic-Static, and Dynamic settings designed to evaluate the model's ability to distinguish and localize moving and stationary regions under different camera and scene motion conditions. MoRSe outperforms both specialized moving object segmentation methods and dense 4D reconstruction methods with motion-thresholded predictions, while producing the fewest false positives on sequences where nothing moves.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31205

40. 【2610.31186】RaSFNet: Ray-aligned and Scan-aware Fusion Network for LiDAR Semantic Segmentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31186

作者：Chuang Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：perception for autonomous, driving and robotics, fundamental component, autonomous driving, Scan-aware Fusion Network

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LiDAR point cloud semantic segmentation is a fundamental component of 3D perception for autonomous driving and robotics. Projection-based methods map 3D point clouds into 2D representations for feature extraction using 2D networks. However, aggregating multiple points during projection can obscure geometric differences within each frustum, while local feature extraction limits the modeling of spatial relationships across scanning regions. Moreover, proximity in the projected representation does not necessarily imply proximity in 3D space, and interactions between point and projected features may mix features from different ranges, reducing semantic discriminability. To address these challenges, we propose the Ray-aligned and Scan-aware Fusion Network (RaSFNet), which incorporates point cloud geometry and LiDAR scanning characteristics into projection-based semantic segmentation. Specifically, the Ray-Aligned Geometric Distribution Encoding (RAGDE) module characterizes point distributions within each frustum using a ray-aligned coordinate system. To model spatial context, a Scan-Topology-Aware Mamba (STAM) block organizes frustum features according to the LiDAR azimuth sampling pattern and captures long-range dependencies across scanning regions. In addition, a Range-Constrained Point-Frustum Fusion (RCPFF) mechanism uses shared range information to guide feature exchange between point and frustum representations while maintaining spatial correspondence. RaSFNet achieves mIoU scores of 70.2% and 77.7% on the SemanticKITTI and nuScenes validation sets, respectively, indicating competitive segmentation performance compared with the evaluated methods.

Abstract:Humanoid loco-manipulation in dynamic scenes is non-Markovian: cues vanish as objects change or are hidden, or leave the view as the robot turns and walks, yet whole-body vision-language-action (VLA) policies see only the present. 44. 【2610.31152】WildFakeToolBench: Benchmarking AI-Generated Image Detectors with Web-Sourced Image Processing

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31152

作者：Mingjie Li,Yukun Jiang,Yicong Tan,Yang Zhang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Cryptography and Security (cs.CR)

关键词：current public forums, software repositories, popular these days, photography tutorials, generations are widely

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:As AI generated images are getting popular these days, discussions on such generations are widely existed in current public forums, software repositories, and photography tutorials. Moreover, with the help of LLM-assisted code translation, malicious actors can adopt such information to evade detectors without specialized knowledge or local GPU execution of the resulting tools. To evaluate such risks, we introduce \textsc{WildFakeToolBench}, a benchmark suite of $156$ executable image-processing tools derived from $220$ public sources across $142$ domains. Each tool retains its source evidence and passes syntax, execution, and output-distortion checks without using feedback from the evaluated detectors. On $105$ generated images from five generators, we evaluate six state-of-the-art detectors with images processed by the tools in our collected bench to assess their effectiveness against such in-the-wild risks. The strongest individual tools achieve attack success rates of $25.25\%$--$100\%$ across detectors, exceeding the strongest of six selected image-processing baselines for each detector. Evaluating subsets of an eight-tool pool yields worst-case coverage above $80\%$ for every detector. Effectiveness is sparse and detector-specific: high coverage across candidates does not imply reliable one-shot evasion. These findings not only highlight the potential of such web-sourced risks, but also show the potential on establishing AI-image detector robustness evaluation framework based on those in-the-wild information.
