99. 【2610.30468】SplatLab: Autonomous Scientific Discovery for 3DGS Research

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30468

作者：Seemandhar Jain,Manmohan Chandraker

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：weeks of expert, expert engineering, Reproducing a published, logged training run, systems read papers

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 14 pages, 4 figures. Project page: [this https URL](https://seemandhar.github.io/splatlab)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reproducing a published 3DGS paper takes weeks of expert engineering before any extension can be proposed. Prior autonomous-research systems read papers, draft hypotheses, and write code, but are limited by short fixed templates, low executability, and weak traceability, and none has been demonstrated end-to-end in a live computer-vision subfield with intense activity. SplatLab is an autonomous research system for 3D Gaussian Splatting that addresses these limitations. Given a research prompt, a target-paper URL, or no input at all, it reproduces a published baseline, proposes an extension, trains and ablates across Mip-NeRF 360 and LLFF, writes the manuscript, and binds every reported number to a logged training run. Five design choices distinguish it: (i) candidates mutate plugins from a verified-reproduction backbone matched to published baselines; (ii) a cascade-weighted Elo tournament across eight islands scores candidates on measured GPU PSNR with verbal-gradient feedback; (iii) a standing falsifier subtracts Elo on failure, integrating stress-test survival into selection; (iv) every reported number is bound to a logged training run, and the run cannot terminate while any claim is unbacked; (v) a seven-category 3DGS taxonomy wraps EVOLVE-BLOCK markers in domain-aligned scaffolds transferable across 3DGS repositories. On PSNR-targeted runs, SplatLab's manuscripts beat their reproduced targets by +0.18 to +3.78 dB, including +3.78 dB on astrophotographic nebula rendering, for which no prior 3DGS method exists. The research cycle compresses from months of graduate-student time to days of mostly autonomous compute. Code, all manuscripts, experiment ledgers, and the cross-run paper store are available on the project page.

100. 【2610.30405】VisCurate: Evaluating Behavioral Equivalence of Visual Skills

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30405

作者：Md Farhan Ishraq,Md Farhan Ishmam,Reaz Hassan Joarder,Md Hasanul Kabir

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Modern vision-based agents, Modern vision-based, skill-based problem-solving, edge detection, vision-based agents

备注： Accepted at the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV 2027), Main Conference

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Modern vision-based agents have shifted toward skill-based problem-solving, where a skill is an executable image-to-image operation, e.g., cropping, thresholding, or edge detection. As these skill libraries grow, they accumulate duplicated, mislabeled, and defective skills. The signals normally used to curate them (a skill's name, description, or source code) say little about how the skill visually changes the image. We present VisCurate, a framework and benchmark that curates visual skill libraries from observed execution rather than from text. VisCurate runs each candidate skill pair on a shared probe battery and assigns one of six behavioral relations, each tied to a library edit that is safe to make. The benchmark injects seven defect types into a clean library at controlled rates, producing 300 reproducible instances with ground-truth relations and repairs. While text baselines accept up to five of six false merges on hard negatives, our verifier accepts none and recovers 51.2% of the pairs that are truly mergeable. On two real, uncurated libraries, VisCurate finds that 92% of the skills a package shares with its downstream copy are redundant, while execution confirms only 10 of 17 published equivalence claims. The curation problem also remains open, as the strongest of the nine automated curators recovers only about half of the repairs. We emphasize that visual skill libraries should be curated by behavior, not by description.

102. 【2610.30347】FiLM-CAM: Keyed Feature Modulation for Conditional-Access Watermarking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30347

作者：Yash Kulthe,Vishal Asnani,Shruti Agarwal,Andrew Gilbert,John Collomosse

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Feature-wise Linear Modulation, keyed feature modulation, feature modulation, enforces authorized encoding, conditional-access image watermarking

备注： Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We propose FiLM-CAM; a conditional-access image watermarking approach that enforces authorized encoding, decoding, and removal through keyed feature modulation. A secret key conditions a Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) mechanism that transforms the internal representation of the watermark signal before embedding, thereby coupling the payload to the key at the feature, rather than pixel level. The same key must be presented to condition the decoder to correctly recover the payload embedded in an image. To enforce conditional access, we train the system to maximize the performance gap between authorized and unauthorized use, ensuring reliable extraction under the correct key while inducing failure under incorrect keys. In addition, we design our encoder to estimate the watermark residual invariant to the presence of pre-existing watermarks. This design enables conditional watermark removal via simple re-encoding and subtraction of the residual using iterative refinement, contingent on knowledge of the secret key. We evaluate FiLM-CAM across multiple watermarking encoder-decoder backbones, demonstrating that keyed feature modulation serves as an effective, architecture-agnostic conditional access module (CAM) for image watermarking.

104. 【2610.30337】MoRE: Motion-Regularized Encoding for Articulated 3D Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30337

作者：Guantian Zheng,Shan Jin

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：image requires recovering, single image requires, image requires, requires recovering, mechanism governing

备注： 6 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generating an articulated object from a single image requires recovering both its appearance and the mechanism governing its parts. A compact encoding of joint attributes does not by itself ensure that reconstruction preserves the resulting motion. We present MoRE, Motion-Regularized Encoding for articulated 3D generation. MoRE represents coordinate-aligned joint lines with axis classification and two axis-orthogonal Plücker moment coefficients, removing the arbitrary choice of an origin along the axis. During representation learning, decoded joint hypotheses are used to move corresponding three-dimensional anchors, and their motions are compared with those of annotated joints. A differentiable expectation over joint types and axis directions couples attribute reconstruction to the motion it induces. An image-conditioned flow learns the resulting functional latents, while a multi-state-adapted Gaussian model supplies appearance. At inference, recovered part assignments and joints articulate a single set of rest-pose Gaussians without regenerating the object for each state. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility and ACD show stronger part-box agreement, articulated-state geometry and visual similarity than the compared baselines, while joint prediction reduces axis, origin and range errors relative to ArtiLatent. Controlled ablations support the joint-line encoding and show that motion supervision reduces anchor-motion error. Code and pretrained checkpoints will be released in the future.

107. 【2610.30319】rraTransfer++: Demonstration-Free End-to-End Planner

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30319

作者：Zikang Xiong,Weixin Li,Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Saarth Bonde,Grantland Hall,Chen Tang,Yihan Hu,Wei Zhan

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：real-world deployments, autonomous driving, fleet-scale driving logs, requires fleet-scale driving, offering diverse behaviors

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:End-to-end autonomous driving has achieved state-of-the-art performance on benchmarks and real-world deployments. Its standard training recipe, however, is expensive across all existing paradigms: imitation learning requires fleet-scale driving logs whose behavior distribution only thinly covers the safety-critical tail; closed-loop reinforcement learning in a photorealistic simulator, while offering diverse behaviors, is bottlenecked by sample-efficiency, per-step cost of rendering, and a forward pass through a large vision backbone. Self-play in vectorized simulators changes the economics: at millions of rollout steps per second, one can obtain a state distribution with natural diversity in collisions, near-misses, and recoveries that no driving log contains, and a highly reactive policy emerges from a basic driving-quality reward. Our approach exploits this asymmetry. We pretrain a single policy by self-play, distill its rollouts into a flow-matching trajectory teacher that also plans, and train lightweight adapters on a frozen pretrained vision backbone to reproduce the latent space of either teacher through the action KL divergence, a batch-relational low-rank structural loss, and, for the planner, a trajectory-matching loss. Every target traces back to the self-play policy, so alignment never supervises against a logged trajectory: a paired dataset of (image, scene-state) frames suffices, with no need for the curated expert demonstrations that imitation pretraining is built on. From this one self-play teacher we obtain two end-to-end students: a closed-loop policy that outperforms every published imitation-based end-to-end method on the photorealistic 3D Gaussian splatting closed-loop benchmark HUGSim, and a planner that reaches 59.4 EPDMS on NAVSIM navhard, above every published method including DriveZero-Scale (57.1), and 95.5 PDMS on navtest, without human demonstrations.

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Signal Processing (eess.SP)

备注： 16 pages, 8 figures, includes supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multibeam imaging sonar produces intensity images degraded by coherent speckle and system-induced blur, while clean reflectivity targets are rarely available. Existing despeckling methods often rely on simplified intensity-domain noise models, synthetic clean targets, or blind-spot self-supervision, and typically do not jointly model deblurring and speckle suppression. We propose a physics-consistent self-supervised framework for joint despeckling and deblurring of multibeam imaging sonar. A stochastic coherent image-formation model yields a conditional-mean forward operator that relates latent scene reflectivity to expected intensity through the squared magnitude of the system point spread function. We then use registered multi-frame sonar sequences to train without clean ground truth images, showing that, under an idealized inter-frame conditional-mean condition, the expected paired loss equals the corresponding forward-prediction loss up to a network-independent constant. To stabilize the ill-conditioned inverse problem, we further adapt robust equivariant imaging using stochastic coherent simulation and introduce a paired forward prediction error metric for ground-truth-free evaluation. Experiments on two real multibeam sonar datasets demonstrate a favourable balance between forward-prediction agreement, homogeneous-region speckle suppression, and preservation of visible structural boundaries compared with representative baselines.

111. 【2610.30966】Bound-Conditioned Latent Inference for Progressive Image Compression

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30966

作者：Jaeseok Jang,Seungmin Jeon,Kwang Pyo Choi,Chang-Su Kim

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：image compression reconstructs, compression reconstructs images, sequential latent inference, involves sequential latent, partial observations

备注： Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Progressive image compression reconstructs images from prefixes of a single bitstream and therefore involves sequential latent inference under partial observations. Existing trit-plane codecs mainly treat decoded trits as discrete symbols for probability prediction, without fully exploiting their role as interval constraints on the underlying continuous latents. We propose bound-conditioned latent inference (BLI), a framework that reformulates progressive trit-plane coding as sequential latent inference under shrinking interval constraints. At each coding step, the trits decoded so far define lower and upper bounds for each latent element. BLI uses the center and width of these bounds, together with hyperprior information shared by the encoder and decoder, to jointly refine the Gaussian parameters $(\mu,\sigma)$ for entropy modeling and the latent estimate $\hat{y}$ for reconstruction. As additional trits are decoded, the feasible intervals shrink monotonically, yielding tighter constraints for the next inference step and enabling flexible in-plane refinement schedules. Experiments on Kodak, CLIC, and JPEG-AI show that BLI achieves state-of-the-art rate-distortion performance among progressive image codecs, reducing BD-rate by $17.78\%$ over DPICT and by $7.96\%$ over CTC on Kodak, while replacing CTC's plane-specific predictors with a shared model that uses about $5.3\times$ fewer parameters and achieves lower latency than CTC.

112. 【2610.30942】GateFormer: Forecast-Space Decomposition for Intra-Hour Solar Irradiance Prediction from Sky Images

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30942

作者：David Hamlyn,Sunny Chaudhary,Jonathon Hare,Tasmiat Rahman

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：storage control increasingly, control increasingly depend, anticipate rapid variability, Sub-hourly dispatch, solar generation

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sub-hourly dispatch, balancing, and storage control increasingly depend on forecasts that anticipate rapid variability in solar generation. Accurate sky-image-based irradiance forecasting requires a combination of visual cloud dynamics with recent irradiance state while adapting to different short-term irradiance-change regimes. To address this, we introduce GateFormer, a GHI-conditioned dual-decoder ViT for intra-hour global horizontal irradiance (GHI) forecasting through forecast-space decomposition. GateFormer embeds observed GHI into the visual token stream, allowing interaction with sky-image patch features through self-attention. From this shared representation, a Trajectory Head predicts future GHI sequence, while a Delta Head predicts final-horizon change from the latest observed GHI. The two final-horizon forecasts are adaptively weighted in prediction space, with magnitude- and direction-supervision targeting the remaining error. On the NREL-TSI benchmark, GateFormer attains 125.01 W/m^2 RMSE at +30 min, improving over a state-of-the-art transformer baseline by 9.6% under paired-timestamp evaluation. Across NREL-TSI, Chilbolton, and SIRTA, GateFormer achieves the lowest final-horizon RMSE among the evaluated baselines, with forecast skill of 22.97-28.86% relative to smart persistence. Component ablations show adaptive weighting outperforms both individual decoders and fixed averaging, distinguishing GateFormer from conventional forecast combination, while the supervised correction further reduces RMSE by 1.68 W/m^2 and MAE by 4.86 W/m^2. Ramp-regime diagnostics show larger gains during operationally important positive ramps, reducing RMSE by 26.27 W/m^2. These results identify forecast-space decomposition as an alternative forecasting formulation, with potential relevance to other multimodal short-horizon forecasting tasks and deployment using only sky images and measured GHI.

113. 【2610.30940】Vision Transformers for Estimating Irradiance Using Data Scarce Sky Images

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30940

作者：David Hamlyn,Sunny Chaudhary,Tasmiat Rahman

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：optimising photovoltaic system, photovoltaic system performance, regions lacking comprehensive, lacking comprehensive ground-based, comprehensive ground-based instrumentation

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Accurate estimation of diffuse horizontal irradiance (DHI) is critical for optimising photovoltaic system performance and energy forecasting yet remains challenging in regions lacking comprehensive ground-based instrumentation. Recent advancements using Vision Transformers (ViTs) trained on extensive sky image datasets have shown promise in replacing costly irradiance measurement equipment, but the scarcity of long-term, high-quality sky imagery significantly restricts practical implementation. Addressing this critical gap, this study proposes a novel dual-framework approach designed for data-scarce scenarios. First, calculated atmospheric parameters, including extraterrestrial irradiance and cyclic time encodings, are integrated to represent sky conditions without utilising any instrumentation. Next, a sequential pipeline initially predicts synthetic global horizontal irradiance (GHI) and uses it as a feature, to refine DHI estimation. Finally, a dual-parallel architecture simultaneously processes raw and overlay-enhanced fisheye sky images. Overlays are generated through unsupervised, physics-informed cloud segmentation to highlight dynamic sky features. Empirical validation is performed using data from the Chilbolton Observatory, chosen for its temperate climate and frequent cloud variability. To simulate data-scarce conditions, models are trained on a single month (e.g., January) and evaluated across a temporally disjoint, full-year test set. Under this setup, the sequential and dual-parallel frameworks achieve RMSE values within 2-3 W/m^2 and 1-6 W/m^2, respectively, of a state-of-the-art ViT trained on the complete dataset. By combining physics-informed modelling with unsupervised segmentation, the proposed method provides a scalable and cost-effective solution for DHI estimation, advancing solar resource assessment in data-constrained environments.

114. 【2610.30910】H-2DGS: Height-Field Gaussian Splatting for Sharp and Deformable Neurosurgical Surface Reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30910

作者：Zhang Zichuan,Bart Brouwers,Rembrandt Bakker,Maud Landers,Geert-Jan Rutten,Maxime Chamberland

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR)

关键词：magnetic resonance imaging, navigation commonly presents, commonly presents magnetic, presents magnetic resonance, Neurosurgical navigation commonly

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Neurosurgical navigation commonly presents magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) as 2D slices, making it difficult to relate the anatomy in those slices to the cortical surface observed through the surgical microscope. We seek a common 3D visualization that combines a photorealistic reconstruction of the exposed cortex during tumor surgery with whole-brain MRI context and diffusion tractography pathways. This requires the reconstructed cortex to form a clear boundary around the brain anatomy rather than a diffuse volume that obscures internal structures. Conventional 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) can reproduce observed views but, under limited surgical view coverage, may distribute opacity through a thick and geometrically ambiguous band. We introduce height-field 2D Gaussian Splatting (H-2DGS), a three-stage pipeline that concentrates a robust 3DGS reconstruction, fuses its depth into a shared truncated signed distance field (TSDF) surface, and represents that surface with curved, surface-oriented Gaussian primitives. Separate height, color, and opacity bases provide local geometry and appearance, while a neighbor-transition objective coordinates overlapping patches. The explicit surface further supports postoperative alignment: MRI-derived depth deforms the reconstruction, and tracked-color supervision with alternating optimization preserves its appearance. Across five real neurosurgical cases, H-2DGS gives low multi-view depth inconsistency, a compact 7.97MB representation, and interactive rendering at 29--42ms per frame. The resulting sharp, deformable surface integrates clearly with tractography and MRI anatomy, including a postoperative resection cavity.
